# IMPERIA COLD WAR — South Korea treaty revalidation and active conditions
KOR_review_valid_df_foreign_purchase_board = {
	country_exists = KOR
	country_exists = USA
	KOR = {
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = USA
				is_embargoing = USA
				is_in_array = { array = imp_dip_sanc_on_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_dip_sanc_by_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_fin_sanc_on_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_fin_sanc_by_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_eco_sanc_on_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_eco_sanc_by_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_mil_sanc_on_us value = USA }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_mil_sanc_by_us value = USA }
			}
		}
	}
	USA = {
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = KOR
				is_embargoing = KOR
				is_in_array = { array = imp_dip_sanc_on_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_dip_sanc_by_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_fin_sanc_on_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_fin_sanc_by_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_eco_sanc_on_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_eco_sanc_by_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_mil_sanc_on_us value = KOR }
				is_in_array = { array = imp_mil_sanc_by_us value = KOR }
			}
		}
	}
	custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = KOR_review_defence_us_stock_tt KOR_review_defence_us_stock_available = yes }
	KOR_review_defence_us_stock_available = yes
	KOR_review_defence_us_stock_available = yes
	KOR = {
		check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MD_skip_treasury_cost }
	}
	USA = {
		check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = MD_skip_treasury_cost }
	}
}
